SpaceAge report for Caste Prime [2].
Turn 5, Year 2021, January 1.

SpaceAge Engine Version: 0.1.144.

Events during turn:
  none.

Bank report:
  Bank account balance: 6287.
  Credit line maximum: 10000.
  Credit rate: 20%, Deposit rate: 5%.

Technology reports:
+ mineral core drilling [cdrill]: Mineral exploitation.
  - core drill [cdrill]: Advanced mining system. A core drill allows you to strip minerals and
    various resources out of the core of any solid body.
+ uranium mining [uminng]: The extraction, purification and refining of pechblend, the basic uranium
  ore from a variety of locations. Uranium mining is used on planetary surfaces and asteroids.
  - unit of uranium [uraniu]: With a half-life of million of years, this is one of the most stable
    of the radio-active elements, and one very easy to use in energy power modules.
+ helium-3 mining [he3min]: The extraction and refining of helium-3 from regolith and gas. Helium-3
  mining can be carried out by any extraction module that has this technology loaded.
  - unit of helium-3 [heliu3]: A light, non-radioactive helium isotope prized as clean fusion fuel;
    scarce on planets but abundant in lunar regolith.
